Second transcription's note: Rule 6 adds $1,000 at age 55 for an unmarried filer or a married filer with self-only coverage all year. A married filer with family coverage gets the additional amount on line 7 instead. Open point: the Note says that for such a married filer the additional amount is not included on line 3, but the chart still gives $5,300 for self-only months. Chose the chart, so self-only months keep the $1,000 and line 7 covers only family months.

Second transcription's note: The worksheet's 'months eligible' are the months in which both items apply: family coverage while eligible, and not in Medicare. Chose months_family for these. When all months qualify, including under the last-month rule with December family coverage, the entry is $1,000 (TIP).

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f8889.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero (used where a line is -0- or not applicable)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: f8889.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One Form 8889 (count of this form)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: person.one}
    note: "The IRS pages do not define this interface. Read as a count of 1 per Form 8889, for counting forms per person. Chose the one-valued Count parameter person.one."

  - id: f8889.h_married
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married at the end of 2025 (filing status married filing jointly or married filing separately)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
    note: "Open point: the instructions use 'married' and 'unmarried' at the end of 2025 for the $1,000 additional amount (line 3 rule 6, the chart, line 7). Chose filing status MFJ or MFS as married. A head of household who is considered unmarried counts as unmarried."

  - id: f8889.h_self
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only coverage contribution limit ($4,300)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.self_only}

  - id: f8889.h_self55
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only coverage limit, age 55 or older ($5,300)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.self_only_55}

  - id: f8889.h_fam
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family coverage contribution limit ($8,550)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.family}

  - id: f8889.h_fam55
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family coverage limit, unmarried and age 55 or older ($9,550)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.family_55}

  - id: f8889.h_self_rate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-only coverage: enter $4,300; if you were age 55 or older at the end of 2025, enter $5,300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_self55
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_self

  - id: f8889.h_fam_rate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Family coverage: enter $8,550; if, at the end of 2025, you were unmarried and age 55 or older, enter $9,550"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_married, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam55
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_married, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam

  - id: f8889.h_months_bad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: family-coverage months exceed eligible months, or eligible months exceed 12"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_family, f8889.months_eligible]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_eligible, {const: 12, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.h_chart_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart: self-only monthly amount times months eligible"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_self_rate, count: f8889.months_eligible}

  - id: f8889.h_chart_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart: self-only monthly amount times family-coverage months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_self_rate, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_chart_c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart: family monthly amount times family-coverage months"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_fam_rate, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_chart_selfmonths
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart: total of amounts for self-only months"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f8889.h_chart_a, take: f8889.h_chart_b}

  - id: f8889.h_chart_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 Limitation Chart: Total for all months"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.h_chart_selfmonths, f8889.h_chart_c]}

  - id: f8889.h_chart
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limitation. Divide the total by 12."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_chart_total, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}
    note: "Medicare months and months not eligible are $0 in the chart. They are excluded because months_eligible counts only eligible months, and an eligible individual is not enrolled in Medicare. The division by 12 is rounded half-up to the cent."

  - id: f8889.h_dec_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Maximum amount that can be contributed based on the type of HDHP coverage you had on the first day of the last month of your tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "self_only"}]}
          then: f8889.h_self_rate
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "family"}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam_rate
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "none"}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero

  - id: f8889.h_chart_or_dec
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The greater of the Line 3 Limitation Chart limitation or the December 1 coverage maximum"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f8889.h_chart, f8889.h_dec_max]}

  - id: f8889.h_use_greater
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Eligible individual on December 1 and eligible (or considered eligible under the last-month rule) for the entire year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ne: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "none"}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.last_month, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.months_eligible, {const: 12, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.h_changed_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 amount when coverage changed during 2025 (rules 4 and 5)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_use_greater, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_chart_or_dec
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_use_greater, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_chart
    note: "Rule 4 (eligible or considered eligible all year, changed coverage) gives the greater of the chart and the December 1 maximum. Rule 5 (not eligible on December 1) gives the chart. A filer eligible on December 1 who does not use the last-month rule and was not eligible all year falls in neither rule; chose the chart alone for that filer."

  - id: f8889.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "If you were under age 55 at the end of 2025 and, on the first day of every month during 2025, you were, or were considered, an eligible individual with the same coverage, enter $4,300 ($8,550 for family coverage). All others, see the instructions for the amount to enter"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "self_only"}]}
          then: f8889.h_self_rate
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
          then: f8889.h_fam_rate
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
          then: f8889.h_changed_limit
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_months_bad, when_known: true}]
    note: "Rule 6 adds $1,000 at age 55 for an unmarried filer or a married filer with self-only coverage all year. A married filer with family coverage gets the additional amount on line 7 instead. Open point: the Note says that for such a married filer the additional amount is not included on line 3, but the chart still gives $5,300 for self-only months. Chose the chart, so self-only months keep the $1,000 and line 7 covers only family months."

  - id: f8889.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Enter the amount you and your employer contributed to your Archer MSAs for 2025 from Form 8853, lines 1 and 2. If you or your spouse had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, also include any amount contributed to your spouse's Archer MSAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Archer MSA contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported, so the supported case is zero."

  - id: f8889.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Subtract line 4 from line 3. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line3, take: f8889.line4}

  - id: f8889.h_family_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You had (or were treated as having) family HDHP coverage at any time during 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f8889.months_family, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8889.h_family_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are treated as having family coverage for each month"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "family"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.months_family, {const: 12, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.coverage_year, {const: "changed"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.last_month, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f8889.dec_coverage, {const: "family"}]}

  - id: f8889.split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You and your spouse each have separate HSAs and had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025 (line 6 special rule applies)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_married, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.hsa_both, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: true}]}
    note: "Whether the spouse also has a separate HSA is read from the published return-level aggregate household.hsa_both, assumed to mean both spouses on the return have a Form 8889. A spouse not on this return (married filing separately) cannot be seen, so the rule is not applied there. This is a limitation of the closed interface."

  - id: f8889.h_fam_part
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 refigured with the worksheet amounts only for the months you were treated as having family coverage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_chart_c, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}
    note: "Open point: Step 1 says to enter on line 3 'the total of the worksheet amounts only for the months' of family coverage. Chose that total divided by 12, as the chart limitation is figured."

  - id: f8889.h_step1_partial
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1 refigured line 5 (family months only), using the same line 4"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.h_fam_part, take: f8889.line4}

  - id: f8889.step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1. The family contribution limit to be divided between spouses (line 5, or line 5 refigured for the family-coverage months only)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.line5
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_step1_partial
    note: "Zero when the spouse rule does not apply. Treated as family coverage each month: line 5 is the amount divided. Otherwise: Step 1 refigured limit."

  - id: f8889.h_half_step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2: the Step 1 limit divided equally between you and your spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.step1, rate: {param: hsa.half}}

  - id: f8889.h_alloc_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2: your part of the Step 1 limit under the allocation you both agree on"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8889.allocated, f8889.step1]}

  - id: f8889.h_alloc_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: the amount allocated to you exceeds the Step 1 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.allocated, f8889.step1]}

  - id: f8889.share_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your allocable share of the family contribution limit (Steps 2 and 3)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "equal"}]}
          then: f8889.h_half_step1
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}
          then: f8889.h_alloc_capped
    refuses: [{when: f8889.h_alloc_over, when_known: true}]
    note: "Step 3 (Step 1 minus the spouse's part) equals your own allocated part, so that part is used directly."

  - id: f8889.h_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: other contribution limits that apply for the tax year (months not treated as family coverage)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line5, take: f8889.step1}
    note: "Read as line 5 less the Step 1 family-month limit, which is the self-only-month amount from the chart (or any greater December 1 amount). It is zero when you are treated as having family coverage every month. Line 4 is zero in the supported case."

  - id: f8889.h_l6_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 total: your allocable share plus other contribution limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.share_raw, f8889.h_other]}

  - id: f8889.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "Enter the amount from line 5. But if you and your spouse each have separate HSAs and had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, see the instructions for the amount to enter"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_l6_split
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.line5

  - id: f8889.h_applies7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age 55 or older at the end of 2025, married, and you or your spouse had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.age55, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_married, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_any, {const: true}]}

  - id: f8889.h_additional
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional contribution amount ($1,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: hsa.additional}

  - id: f8889.h_l7_months_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional Contribution Amount Worksheet line 1: $1,000 × number of months eligible"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: f8889.h_additional, count: f8889.months_family}

  - id: f8889.h_l7_partial
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional Contribution Amount Worksheet line 2: divide line 1 by 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_l7_months_amt, rate: {param: hsa.twelfth}}

  - id: f8889.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "If you were age 55 or older at the end of 2025, married, and you or your spouse had family coverage under an HDHP at any time during 2025, enter your additional contribution amount. See instructions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_applies7, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_applies7, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_additional
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_applies7, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_family_all, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_l7_partial
    note: "The worksheet's 'months eligible' are the months in which both items apply: family coverage while eligible, and not in Medicare. Chose months_family for these. When all months qualify, including under the last-month rule with December family coverage, the entry is $1,000 (TIP)."

  - id: f8889.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Add lines 6 and 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.line6, f8889.line7]}

  - id: f8889.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Qualified HSA funding distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "Qualified HSA funding distributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported, so the supported case is zero."

  - id: f8889.line11
    printed: "11"
    label: "Add lines 9 and 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.line9, f8889.line10]}

  - id: f8889.line12
    printed: "12"
    label: "Subtract line 11 from line 8. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line8, take: f8889.line11}

  - id: f8889.h_l13_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of line 2 or line 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8889.line2, f8889.line12]}

  - id: f8889.line13
    printed: "13"
    label: "HSA deduction (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line2, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line2, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_l13_min
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]
    note: "The smaller of line 2 or line 12. When line 2 is zero this is zero whatever line 12 is. Prior-year excess contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported."

  - id: f8889.h_excess_own
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excess contributions you make: line 2 minus line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line2, take: f8889.line13}

  - id: f8889.h_excess_employer
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excess employer contributions: employer contributions over your limitation on line 8 (reduced by any qualified HSA funding distribution)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line11, take: f8889.line8}

  - id: f8889.excess_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "2025 excess HSA contributions (made by you, on your behalf, or by your employer) over your contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.h_excess_own, f8889.h_excess_employer]}
    note: "The IRS pages do not name this interface. Read as the 2025 excess for Form 5329 line 47: line 2 over the limit, which equals line 2 minus line 13, plus any excess employer contributions. Withdrawals of excess contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported."

  - id: f8889.h_employer_excess_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excess employer contributions not included in income on Form W-2 (reported as Other income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    display: "The instructions say that if the excess was not included in income on Form W-2, you must report it as Other income. Shown for that report."
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.h_excess_employer, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.h_excess_employer, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.employer_in_box1, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.h_excess_employer, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.employer_in_box1, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_excess_employer

  - id: f8889.h_rollover_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: the amount rolled over exceeds total distributions on line 14a"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.rollover, f8889.line14a]}

  - id: f8889.line14b
    printed: "14b"
    label: "Distributions included on line 14a that you rolled over to another HSA. Also include any excess contributions (and the earnings on those excess contributions) included on line 14a that were withdrawn by the due date of your return. See instructions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.rollover
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_rollover_over, when_known: true}]
    note: "Withdrawn excess contributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported, so only rollovers are entered. With no distributions the line is zero."

  - id: f8889.line14c
    printed: "14c"
    label: "Subtract line 14b from line 14a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line14a, take: f8889.line14b}
    note: "The form gives no zero floor. Line 14b cannot exceed line 14a (consistency check on line 14b), so the floor never changes the value; it is used only to keep the NonNegDollars type."

  - id: f8889.h_parts
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions rolled over plus distributions used for qualified medical expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8889.rollover, f8889.medical]}

  - id: f8889.h_parts_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: rolled-over and medical parts together exceed total distributions on line 14a"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.h_parts, f8889.line14a]}

  - id: f8889.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Qualified medical expenses paid using HSA distributions (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, gt: [f8889.line14a, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.medical
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_parts_over, when_known: true}]

  - id: f8889.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions. Subtract line 15 from line 14c. If zero or less, enter -0-. Also, include this amount in the total on Schedule 1 (Form 1040), Part I, line 8f"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8889.line14c, take: f8889.line15}
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported]

  - id: f8889.h_subject
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount included on line 16 that does not meet any of the exceptions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8889.subject_part, f8889.line16]}

  - id: f8889.h_subject_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: the part subject to the additional tax exceeds line 16"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8889.subject_part, f8889.line16]}

  - id: f8889.h_tax_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "20% (0.20) of line 16 (no exception applies)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.line16, rate: {param: hsa.additional_rate}}

  - id: f8889.h_tax_part
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "20% (0.20) of the part of line 16 that does not meet any of the exceptions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8889.h_subject, rate: {param: hsa.additional_rate}}

  - id: f8889.line17b
    printed: "17b"
    label: "Additional 20% tax (see instructions). Enter 20% (0.20) of the distributions included on line 16 that are subject to the additional 20% tax. Also, include this amount in the total on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), Part II, line 17c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
          then: f8889.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.line17a, {const: false}]}
          then: f8889.h_tax_all
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, gt: [f8889.line16, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f8889.line17a, {const: true}]}
          then: f8889.h_tax_part
    refuses: [f8889.unsupported, {when: f8889.h_subject_over, when_known: true}]
    note: "The death exception and returned mistaken distributions are screened out by f8889.unsupported. Disability and age 65 are covered by line 17a and subject_part."
```
```

<!-- end -->
